[basemain_continuous.py] class PvtV2B4_FPN_BCSR_NC_Curriculum(
    PvtV2B4_FPN_CSR_NC_Curriculum
):
    """B3: box-supervised CSR with conservative residual release.

    The model still consumes RGB only at inference. ``box_mask`` is used only
    to train the scale router and reliable exterior-background constraint.
    """

    def __init__(self, pretrained=True, input_norm=True, fpn_dim=64,
                 csr_groups=4, use_checkpoint=False, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(
            pretrained=pretrained,
            input_norm=input_norm,
            fpn_dim=fpn_dim,
            csr_groups=csr_groups,
            use_checkpoint=use_checkpoint,
            q_switch_ratio=0.75,
            alpha_schedule="boxscale",
            router_freeze_ratio=0.67,
            box_scale_lambda=0.20,
            outside_lambda=0.05,
            box_required=True,
            **kwargs,
        )
        self.nc_loss = NoisyCODCurriculumLoss(
            q_switch_ratio=0.75,
            continuous_q=True,
        )
